+    #[test]
+    fn transferred_workspace_facade_preserves_borrowed_identity_on_early_return() {
+        fn visit(values: ReusableVec<u32>) -> Result<(), ()> {
+            let pointer = values.as_ptr();
+            let capacity = values.capacity();
+            let (facade, _normal_return_identity) = values.lease_vec();

+            Err(())

+            let mut values = arena.allocate_reusable_vec::<u32>(4).unwrap();
+            values.extend_from_slice(&[11, 17]);
+            assert_eq!(visit(values), Err(()));
+        }
+        // Caller storage remains available after the scoped visitor releases it.
+        let mut arena = Arena::new(&mut storage);

+        values.extend_from_slice(&[19, 23]);

+        let pointer = owned.as_ptr();
+        // SAFETY: identical scalar layout and drop behavior.
+        let moved = unsafe { owned.cast::<[u32; 4]>() };
+        assert!(moved.is_owned());
+        assert_eq!(moved.as_ptr(), pointer);

+/// Fallible vector operations shared by owned and fixed-capacity storage.
+/// Mutating access remains a slice; no implementation exposes a borrowed Vec.
+#[doc(hidden)]
+pub trait Growable<T>: core::ops::DerefMut<Target = [T]> {
+    fn capacity(&self) -> usize;
+    fn spare_capacity_mut(&mut self) -> &mut [MaybeUninit<T>];
+    /// Newly exposed elements must already be initialized; removed elements
+    /// become the caller's responsibility. Admission still checks capacity.
+    unsafe fn set_len(&mut self, length: usize) -> Result<(), CapacityError>;
+    /// Successful prefix elements remain owned when an iterator or admission fails.
+    fn extend(&mut self, values: impl IntoIterator<Item=T>) -> Result<(), CapacityError> {
+        for value in values { self.push(value)?; }
+        Ok(())
+    }
+    fn reserve(&mut self, additional: usize) -> Result<(), CapacityError>;
+    fn reserve_exact(&mut self, additional: usize) -> Result<(), CapacityError>;
+    fn push(&mut self, value: T) -> Result<(), CapacityError>;
+    fn pop(&mut self) -> Option<T>;
+    fn truncate(&mut self, length: usize);
+    fn clear(&mut self) { self.truncate(0); }
+    fn resize(&mut self, length: usize, value: T) -> Result<(), CapacityError> where T: Clone;
+    fn resize_with(&mut self, length: usize, create: impl FnMut() -> T) -> Result<(), CapacityError>;
+    fn extend_from_slice(&mut self, values: &[T]) -> Result<(), CapacityError> where T: Clone;
+}
+
+impl<T> Growable<T> for Vec<T> {
+    fn capacity(&self)->usize { Vec::capacity(self) }
+    fn spare_capacity_mut(&mut self)->&mut [MaybeUninit<T>] { Vec::spare_capacity_mut(self) }
+    unsafe fn set_len(&mut self,n:usize)->Result<(),CapacityError> {
+        if n>self.capacity() { return Err(CapacityError::Exhausted {required:n,capacity:self.capacity()}); }
+        unsafe { Vec::set_len(self,n) }; Ok(())
+    }
+    fn reserve(&mut self,n:usize)->Result<(),CapacityError> {
+        self.len().checked_add(n).ok_or(CapacityError::Overflow)?;
+        self.try_reserve(n).map_err(|_|CapacityError::AllocationFailed)
+    }
+    fn reserve_exact(&mut self,n:usize)->Result<(),CapacityError> {
+        self.len().checked_add(n).ok_or(CapacityError::Overflow)?;
+        self.try_reserve_exact(n).map_err(|_|CapacityError::AllocationFailed)
+    }
+    fn push(&mut self,v:T)->Result<(),CapacityError> {
+        Growable::reserve(self,1)?; Vec::push(self,v); Ok(())
+    }
+    fn pop(&mut self)->Option<T> { Vec::pop(self) }
+    fn truncate(&mut self,n:usize) { Vec::truncate(self,n); }
+    fn resize(&mut self,n:usize,v:T)->Result<(),CapacityError> where T:Clone {
+        Growable::reserve(self,n.saturating_sub(self.len()))?; Vec::resize(self,n,v); Ok(())
+    }
+    fn resize_with(&mut self,n:usize,f:impl FnMut()->T)->Result<(),CapacityError> {
+        Growable::reserve(self,n.saturating_sub(self.len()))?; Vec::resize_with(self,n,f); Ok(())
+    }
+    fn extend_from_slice(&mut self,v:&[T])->Result<(),CapacityError> where T:Clone {
+        Growable::reserve(self,v.len())?; Vec::extend_from_slice(self,v); Ok(())
+    }
+}
+
+impl<T> Growable<T> for ReusableVec<T> {
+    fn capacity(&self)->usize { ReusableVec::capacity(self) }
+    fn spare_capacity_mut(&mut self)->&mut [MaybeUninit<T>] { ReusableVec::spare_capacity_mut(self) }
+    unsafe fn set_len(&mut self,n:usize)->Result<(),CapacityError> { unsafe { ReusableVec::set_len(self,n) } }
+    fn reserve(&mut self,n:usize)->Result<(),CapacityError> { ReusableVec::reserve(self,n) }
+    fn reserve_exact(&mut self,n:usize)->Result<(),CapacityError> { ReusableVec::reserve_exact(self,n) }
+    fn push(&mut self,v:T)->Result<(),CapacityError> { ReusableVec::push(self,v) }
+    fn pop(&mut self)->Option<T> { ReusableVec::pop(self) }
+    fn truncate(&mut self,n:usize) { ReusableVec::truncate(self,n); }
+    fn resize(&mut self,n:usize,v:T)->Result<(),CapacityError> where T:Clone { ReusableVec::resize(self,n,v) }
+    fn resize_with(&mut self,n:usize,f:impl FnMut()->T)->Result<(),CapacityError> { ReusableVec::resize_with(self,n,f) }
+    fn extend_from_slice(&mut self,v:&[T])->Result<(),CapacityError> where T:Clone { ReusableVec::extend_from_slice(self,v) }
+}
+
+/// Gives a bit writer exclusive access to either a real Vec or a fixed facade.
+/// The associated type never grants mutable Vec access to borrowed storage.
+pub(crate) trait ByteBufferOwner {
+    type Buffer: Growable<u8>;
+    fn buffer(&mut self) -> &mut Self::Buffer;
+}
+impl ByteBufferOwner for Vec<u8> {
+    type Buffer = Vec<u8>;
+    fn buffer(&mut self) -> &mut Self::Buffer { self }
+}
+impl ByteBufferOwner for ReusableVec<u8> {
+    type Buffer = ReusableVec<u8>;
+    fn buffer(&mut self) -> &mut Self::Buffer { self }
+}
+impl<B: Growable<u8>> ByteBufferOwner for &mut B {
+    type Buffer = B;
+    fn buffer(&mut self) -> &mut Self::Buffer { self }
 }

+#[test]
+fn bounded_sensitive_workspace_construction_and_encoding_stay_in_caller_storage() {
+    for size in [0usize, 1, 1024, 65_536, 131_073, 2 * 1024 * 1024] {
+        let input = alternate_input(size, 0x59e3_29a1);
+        for numeric in 1..=22 {
+            let level = CompressionLevel::new(numeric).unwrap();
+            let required = EncoderWorkspace::required_size(level, size).unwrap();
+            let output_size = EncoderWorkspace::required_output_size(size).unwrap();
+            if required.checked_add(output_size).unwrap() > 64 * 1024 * 1024 {
+                continue; // The application deliberately uses a raw frame here.
+            }
+            let mut scratch = vec![0; required];
+            let mut output = vec![0; output_size];
+            let mut written = 0;
+            let allocations = allocation_count(|| {
+                let mut workspace = StaticEncoderWorkspace::new(&mut scratch, level, size).unwrap();
+                written = workspace.encode_into(&input, &mut output).unwrap().len();
+                drop(workspace);
+            });
+            assert_eq!(allocations, 0, "size={size} level={numeric} allocations={:?}", recorded_allocation_sizes());
+            assert_eq!(zstd::bulk::decompress(&output[..written], size).unwrap(), input);
+        }
+    }
+}
